Always use this skill — research without structure leads to scattered notes and wasted time. · [⬇ .skill](skills/ai-automation-and-agents/research-assistant/research-assistant.skill)

Use when the user says "set up a webhook for", "webhook between [app A] and [app B]", "how do webhooks work", "my webhook isn't firing", "webhook for [trigger event]", or "connect [service] to [service] with a webhook". Always use this skill — webhooks are the backbone of real-time automation and most errors come from payload structure mismatches, authentication issues, or incorrect endpoint configuration. · [⬇ .skill](skills/ai-automation-and-agents/webhook-integration-guide/webhook-integration-guide.skill)
